# wzSearch – data-quality report

Generated `2026-11-25T00:15:15Z` by `pipeline/build.py` from the district's [source index](https://bialoleka.um.warszawa.pl/decyzje-architektoniczne), the [BAiPP lists](https://architektura.um.warszawa.pl/postepowania-i-decyzje) and GUNB's [RWDZ register](https://wyszukiwarka.gunb.gov.pl/pobranie.html). Regenerated on every build; do not edit.

## Totals

* Records per dataset: Dzielnica 6410, BAiPP (miasto) 172, GUNB RWDZ 5953 – `records.json` 6582, `permits.json` 5953.
* Monthly pages: **3** (– → –); source files: **25** (23 with rows, 0 empty templates, 2 unreadable/missing).
* Workbook data rows kept (district + BAiPP): **7355** → records after merging duplicates: **6582** (WZ 2707, CP 423, Wszczęcie 3452).
* Building permits and notifications (RWDZ): **5953** (Pozwolenie 4817, Zgłoszenie 1136).
* Records listed in more than one source row: 766 ([`ws-bia-n-rpw-176604-2026`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/ws-bia-n-rpw-176604-2026?dz=bialoleka), [`ws-bia-n-rpw-167344-2026`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/ws-bia-n-rpw-167344-2026?dz=bialoleka), [`ws-bia-n-rpw-153757-2026`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/ws-bia-n-rpw-153757-2026?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-332`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-332?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-331`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-331?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-330`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-330?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-329`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-329?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-328`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-328?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-327`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-327?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-326`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-326?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-325`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-325?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-324`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-324?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-323`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-323?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-322`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-322?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-321`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-321?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-320`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-320?dz=bialoleka), [`ws-bia-n-rpw-132823-2026`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/ws-bia-n-rpw-132823-2026?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-319`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-319?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-318`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-318?dz=bialoleka), [`wz-bia-2026-317`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-2026-317?dz=bialoleka)).

## Declared vs parsed counts

Each monthly page states how many entries each list has ("3 pozycje"). `parsed` counts the data rows read from the files first published on that page (a leftover link to an earlier month's file is not counted twice).

| Kind | Declared (blocks with a number) | Parsed in those blocks | Parsed in blocks without a number | Parsed rows | Duplicates merged | Records |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| WZ | 0 | 0 | 2636 | 2968 | 332 | 2636 |
| CP | 0 | 0 | 403 | 403 | 0 | 403 |
| Wszczęcie | 0 | 0 | 3381 | 3799 | 428 | 3371 |

All blocks with a declared count match the parsed files.

## Completeness: gaps in decision numbering

WZ and CP decisions are numbered consecutively per year, so a number that never appears in any file is a decision that was not published in these lists (or a refusal/discontinuation the office does not list). Numbering restarts every year at 1; in the first year only numbers after the lowest one seen count, because the publication started in August 2020. (District numbering only: BAiPP numbers its decisions city-wide.)

| Kind | Year | Published | Range seen | Missing | Missing numbers |
|---|---|---:|---|---:|---|

## GUNB RWDZ (building permits and notifications)

GUNB publishes its register of building-permit decisions and notifications (*Rejestr wniosków, decyzji i zgłoszeń*, CC0 1.0) as three ZIP files rebuilt every night. Every case with a parcel in Praga-Południe (cadastral unit `146507_8`) is copied byte for byte into a committed extract (`data/raw/rwdz/`, mirrored to `site/sources/rwdz/`) and becomes one record in `permits.json`; the snapshot cited is the one the extract was taken from (a new GUNB file that changes nothing in the district is not adopted).

| File | Kind | Snapshot (Last-Modified) | Rows in the file | Praga-Południe rows | Records | Extract |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| [wynik_mazowieckie.zip](https://wyszukiwarka.gunb.gov.pl/pliki_pobranie/wynik_mazowieckie.zip) | Pozwolenie | 2026-10-26T22:35:07Z | 695711 | 10683 | 4817 | [`sources/d/bialoleka/rwdz/rwdz-wnioski.csv`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/sources/d/bialoleka/rwdz/rwdz-wnioski.csv) |
| [wynik_zgloszenia_2016_2021.zip](https://wyszukiwarka.gunb.gov.pl/pliki_pobranie/wynik_zgloszenia_2016_2021.zip) | Zgłoszenie | 2026-10-26T22:36:03Z | 829354 | 2295 | 574 | [`sources/d/bialoleka/rwdz/rwdz-zgloszenia-2016-2021.csv`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/sources/d/bialoleka/rwdz/rwdz-zgloszenia-2016-2021.csv) |
| [wynik_zgloszenia_2022_up.zip](https://wyszukiwarka.gunb.gov.pl/pliki_pobranie/wynik_zgloszenia_2022_up.zip) | Zgłoszenie | 2026-10-26T22:36:07Z | 939616 | 2232 | 562 | [`sources/d/bialoleka/rwdz/rwdz-zgloszenia-2022.csv`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/sources/d/bialoleka/rwdz/rwdz-zgloszenia-2022.csv) |

* Scope of works: Pozwolenie: Budowa nowego obiektu 3248, Inne roboty budowlane (np. przebudowa, remont) 1127, Rozbiórka 247, Rozbudowa 176, Nadbudowa 17, nie podano 1, Odbudowa 1; Zgłoszenie: Budowa nowego obiektu 1115, Rozbudowa 21.

Register rows of the district office (*Prezydent Miasta Warszawa - Praga Płd.*) with a broken cadastral unit are kept and corrected to `146507_8` (a warning on the record), and so are the rows of the cases in `rwdz.UNIT_FIXES` (`listed`: register errors checked one by one in ULDK, any organ); a valid other Warsaw unit two or more edits away is genuine and not kept:

* `rwdz-wnioski`: kept – empty 1, typo 14; not kept – none.
* `rwdz-zgloszenia-2016-2021`: kept – typo 6; not kept – none.
* `rwdz-zgloszenia-2022`: kept – none; not kept – none.
* Records with a corrected unit: 1 ([`pb-st-mz-bi-2016-4560`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/pb-st-mz-bi-2016-4560?dz=bialoleka)).

## Related records across datasets

`related` links records that share a cadastral parcel or a street + house number, across all three datasets. A shared parcel is not necessarily the same project (a large housing-estate parcel carries many unrelated renovations).

* Dzielnica: 2590 of 6410 records relate to at least one permit or notification.
* BAiPP (miasto): 96 of 172 records relate to at least one permit or notification.
* GUNB RWDZ: 1517 of 5953 permits/notifications relate to a WZ/ULICP record.
* WZ decisions (not refused/discontinued) followed by a building permit on the same parcel: 122 of 650 (median 334 days to the first such permit).
* CP decisions (not refused/discontinued) followed by a building permit on the same parcel: 46 of 168 (median 178 days to the first such permit).
* Most related: [`wz-bia-f1028efd855-r6`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-f1028efd855-r6?dz=bialoleka) (185), [`wz-bia-f533910591d-r21`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-f533910591d-r21?dz=bialoleka) (185), [`wz-bia-febcdbc77a7-r39`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/wz-bia-febcdbc77a7-r39?dz=bialoleka) (141), [`cp-bia-f4b211b4821-r30`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/cp-bia-f4b211b4821-r30?dz=bialoleka) (138), [`cp-bia-f19d688fd7a-r39`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/cp-bia-f19d688fd7a-r39?dz=bialoleka) (138).
